Plan for Declaration adaptive-fit (recorded 2026-10-10T21:06:37-04:00), file TrilinearHT/Auto/SecBaseReductionFits.lean (blueprint §11.2, authorized split):
- formSmall w I j r q η: every progression form at I with r in slot j, one normalized L^q input and two normalized L^2 inputs in the other slots is at most η (this is d_{j,I}(r) ≤ η).
- Labels V = Fin M, M = path cap ~ 8ρ^{-2}. All labels are born at the attempt root; label m on the path of y is the m-th inserted atom on that path. Disjoint branches reuse labels with piecewise scalars, so path counts are ≤ M uniformly. Masks are {m < |L_c|}.
- The construction uses local ranges span{1_c (φ_i e_phys + e_i)} in ColumnL2 (Fin M), without ColumnTreeData. Per cell, the greedy step inserts the detection atom while not small and |L| < M. Atoms on a child are those of the parent followed by its own greedy insertions.
- Energy: each insertion gains ≥ ρ²|c|/2 (fresh coordinate, column norm ≤ √2). Inherited ranges of the children contain the parent's range, so energies telescope over depth and Σ_c |c| n_c ≤ 2|J|/ρ².
- Stuck cells (not small, cap reached) are first crossings; their total length is ≤ |J|/4, and the root is never stuck. Restarts recurse on depth, giving total root length ≤ 4/3 |J|.

Plan for prop:base (recorded 2026-10-10T22:20:16-04:00): (1) done: adaptive-fit attempts packaged as ColumnDepthFamily rooted at any tree cell R below the attempt root K (FitSetup.depthFamily K R), so low fits are admissible opponents renormalized at R. For any tree cell R the system gives a ColumnDepthFamily rooted at R whose cell projections are root-independent; this lets lem:delay be applied at each low pair's joint birth cell J' with energies localized to J', and Cauchy-Schwarz over the antichain of joint birth cells gives Σ_J' ‖1_J' F_a‖‖1_J' F_b‖ ≤ ‖1_R F_a‖‖1_R F_b‖. Predictor labels are (low label pair, predictor index re-indexed into Fin D_pred).
